美時 (1795 TT)  Trading Buy

2026 年毛利率回升，海外 ECB 再融資計畫落地將降低利
息負擔。  
投資建議 ：(1) Alvogen 併購效益與全球 B2B 業務，產品組合優
化趨勢確立。營收規模翻倍且產品組合改善帶動毛利率回升；
(2) 高壁壘產品線進入密集收割期 ， 重磅 505(b)(2) Cabozantinib
預計 4Q26 審查完成。 (3) 預估 2026 EPS 12.08 元，投資建議
Trading Buy ，目標價 200 元。  
2Q26 在併購營收規模擴張下，毛利率與獲利修復動能，但淨利
仍受到併購融資之高額財務利息與一次性稅負壓抑：  
2Q26 營收 88.09 億，毛利率為 53.68% ，較 1Q26 的 50.73% 提
升，主要反映高毛利產品出貨回升與產品組合轉佳。費用顯著
攀升主要係合併 Alvogen 後營運規模擴張所致。  
營業外收支部分， 1H26 非營業費用 25.76 億，其中財務成本達
23.67 億 (YoY+980.82%) ，主要受到美國較高利率環境下產生龐
大財務利息成本，以及期間美元升值產生之外幣匯兌損失衝擊。
所得稅方面， 1H26 認列非經常性一次性稅務項目，若排除該項
一次性影響，調整後有效稅率為 22.3% 。  
2H26 美時將以 ECB 再融資顯著減輕利息成本為獲利修復核心 ：  
受惠於併購 Alvogen 後的完整年度營收挹注 、 7 月起併入 Sandoz
菲律賓業務、韓國 Uro-Vaxom 經銷啟動，以及 Nintedanib 與
Enzalutamide 等 B2B 產品持續在全球多國鋪貨出貨， 2026 全年
營收將維持翻倍增長曲線。隨著 2Q26 產品組合優化使毛利率
回升至 53.7% ，預期 2H26 在高毛利癌症藥物與自製困難學名藥
出貨比例提升下，全年毛利率將維持在 52%–54% 區間。  
美時海外可轉換公司債 (ECB) 發行案已正式獲得證期局核准，將
積極推進債務再融資計畫，目標是大幅置換現有高利率貸款、
顯著降低下半年及未來的財務利息費用，進一步釋放獲利彈性
並提升資金調度靈活度。

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 9 月 2 日

2 群益投顧 
  ESG ：  
美時之企業使命「提供全球病患更好、更安全且更負擔得起的藥物」。為追
求使命，同時投入環境與社會之永續發展， 2024 年初，美時已成立「永續
暨風險管理小組」 ，針對公司的永續發展工作建立管理機制，研擬永續發展
相關之短、中、長期目標、策略，並管理監督跨部門之執行過程與成果，以
從系統性的內部管理，極小化美時產品對於社會及環境所帶來的潛在衝擊。
公司簡介：  
美時專精於高門檻的複雜學名藥及癌症用藥的研發、生產與銷售。公司產品
線涵蓋中樞神經系統、癌症、心血管疾病等領域，並在美國、歐洲及亞洲多
國市場擁有廣泛的銷售通路。  
透過策略性併購與全球市場的布局，美時已逐步從區域性藥廠轉型為具備
全球影響力的國際製藥公司。  
2Q26 在併購營收規模擴張下，毛利率與獲利修復動能，但淨利仍受到併購
融資之高額財務利息與一次性稅負壓抑：  
2Q26 營收 88.09 億， 1H26 累計營收 174.53 億 (YoY+84.18%) ，創下歷史半
年度新高紀錄。 2Q26 毛利率為 53.68% ，較 1Q26 的 50.73% 提升 3 個百分
點，主要反映高毛利產品出貨回升與產品組合轉佳。 1H26 營業費用合計
48.22 億 (YoY+93.65%) ，其中管銷費用為 42.96 億 (YoY+97.06%) 、研發費用
為 5.26 億 (YoY+69.68%) ，費用顯著攀升主要係合併 Alvogen 後營運規模擴
張所致。  
營業外收支部分 ，1H26 非營業費用合計高達 25.76 億 ， 其中財務成本達23.67
億 (YoY+980.82%) ，其他營業外淨支出為 2.09 億 (YoY-51.40%) ，主要受到美
國較高利率環境下產生龐大財務利息成本，以及期間美元升值產生之外幣
匯兌損失衝擊。所得稅方面， 1H26 認列非經常性一次性稅務項目，使本期
實際有效稅率暫時提升至 29.5% ；若排除該項一次性影響，調整後有效稅率
為 22.3% 。  
區域營收佔比與年增：  
市場區域 1H26 佔
比 1H26 金額變動(YoY) 變動說明 
美國 62% +68.97 億(+180%YoY) 併購 Alvogen 業務 
韓國 17% -0.27 億(微幅下滑) 既有成熟產品線自然波動 
B2B 業務 8% +10.42 億(+245%YoY) Nintedanib及 Enzalutamide全
球授權夥伴拉貨爆發 
台灣 7% -0.35 億(微幅下滑) 健保藥價定期調整 
東南亞 5% +0.78 億 區域通路擴展及產品送件核
准放量 
亞洲其他 1% +0.21 億 新興市場授權合作 
1H26 營收在地理分佈上呈現重大轉變，美國市場在 Alvogen 完整合併後營
收大幅增長 180% 達 68.97 億，營收佔比攀升至 62% ，成為公司最核心的營
運市場； B2B 全球外銷業務表現極為亮眼，受惠於 Nintedanib 於歐洲 27 國、
加拿大、台灣與美國上市出貨，以及 Enzalutamide 於加拿大、巴西、以色列

3 群益投顧 
與韓國上市放量， B2B 營收暴增 245% 達 10.42 億，佔比升至 8%；亞洲既有
市場整體保持平穩 (+1%YoY) ，其中東南亞增加 0.78 億、亞洲其他增加 0.21
億，有效抵銷韓國 (-0.27 億 )與台灣 (-0.35 億 )的微幅回檔。  
2H26 美時將以 ECB 再融資顯著減輕利息成本為獲利修復核心：  
受惠於併購 Alvogen 後的完整年度營收挹注、 7 月起併入 Sandoz 菲律賓業
務、韓國 Uro-Vaxom 經銷啟動，以及 Nintedanib 與 Enzalutamide 等 B2B 產
品持續在全球多國鋪貨出貨， 2026 全年營收將維持翻倍增長曲線。在毛利
率表現上，隨著 2Q26 產品組合優化使毛利率回升至 53.7% ，預期 2H26 在
高毛利癌症藥物與自製困難學名藥出貨比例提升下，全年毛利率將維持在
52%–54% 區間。  
美時海外可轉換公司債 (ECB) 發行案已正式獲得證期局核准，將積極推進債
務再融資計畫，目標是大幅置換現有高利率貸款、顯著降低下半年及未來的
財務利息費用，進一步釋放獲利彈性並提升資金調度靈活度。  
中長期來看，管線中超過三分之一的專案將於 2026 至 2028 年密集推出，產
品上市能見度極高，將推動公司在 2026 年之後維持成長動能。  
投資建議 ：(1) Alvogen 併購效益與全球 B2B 業務，產品組合優化趨勢確立。
營收規模翻倍且產品組合改善帶動毛利率回升； (2) 高壁壘產品線進入密集
收割期，重磅 505(b)(2) Cabozantinib 預計 4Q26 審查完成。 (3) 預估 2026 
EPS 12.08 元，投資建議 Trading Buy ，目標價 200 元。

                                        2026 年 9 月 2 日

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

From Enterprise AI Adoption to Semicap
Equipment: Sizing the AI Build-out
SEMICON TAIWAN 2026 | MARKET AND INDUSTRY TREND FORUM
Rolf Bulk
Director of Research
RBulk@ETR.ai · +65 6230 3419
September 1, 2026
ABOUT ETR
Enterprise Technology Research (ETR) is an enterprise technology market research firm that delivers
actionable, transparent, and unbiased insights to institutional investors, drawing on proprietary surveys of
a trusted community of nearly 10,000 technology leaders. For more than 15 years, ETR's data has

empowered its clients to make smarter, faster decisions. ETR is a Futurum Group company. Learn more
at etr.ai.
ABOUT THE FUTURUM GROUP
The Futurum Group is a technology research, intelligence, and advisory firm. Futurum Intelligence, the
research arm of The Futurum Group, is led by analysts, researchers, and advisors who help business
leaders worldwide anticipate tectonic shifts in their industries and leverage disruptive innovation. Learn
more at futurumgroup.com.

ML / AI adoption is increasing: pervasion amongst
1,636 enterprises surveyed tripled in four years to
62.6%.
The largest buyers are ahead of the curve: Fortune
100 pervasion is 71%, up 10 points over the year.

No winner takes all dynamic: including pilots,
Claude and Copilot are in use or evaluated by 88%
of enterprises.
Room for higher penetration: Claude is piloted by
35% of enterprises vs. 17% for Copilot, with more of
its base still to convert.

Statements posed to IT decision makers on AI budget and vendor choice.
AI returns are improving: positive ROI of 51% in
July, the highest of eight surveys, up from 46% in
late 2024.
Investments are rationalizing: the share of
enterprises not measuring ROI came down from

27% to 20%.

have new AI budget available next year.
AI is prioritized in vendor selection: AI features
drive 64% of renewals and 60% of new deals.
Budget is cut from other tech areas:61% of
enterprises reallocate budget from other areas.

N=297 who reallocate IT budget to cover AI overruns. Multi-select.
Half the market is over budget:47% run above
plan, 32% in line, and only 6% came in under budget.
Enterprises don't slow down: 48% seek extra
budget and 43% reconcile budgets next cycle. Only
17% pause or scale back AI deployments.

Contractors are cut first: external contractors and
consultants absorb 61% of reallocations.
Hardware refresh is delayed:49% of companies
push out refresh cycles, followed by non-AI
software and headcount.

to have hard limits within three months.

Open weights are already in production:47% of

performance, 57% of pilots would switch for a
discount of 25% or less.

workloads: among those already in production,
74% would switch for 25% or less.

Macro Views, July 2026 (N=1,636). Hardware covers all purchasing categories, AI parts included.
Budget is the main constraint, followed by
memory:41% of buyers name budget as the #1
constraint in deploying more compute; memory is
#2, well ahead of GPUs.

Hardware inflation shows up in enterprise
reporting: hardware pricing up 6.9% half-on-half in
July, from 3.6% in January, while cloud and software
were stable in a 2 to 4% band.
Memory is ahead of other segments:80% of
memory buyers report price increases, and 26% of
those saw 50% or more.

Total datacenter capex, AI and general purpose. Shares may not sum to 100 because of rounding.
Capex per gigawatt is set to increase: stable at
$35B to $36B in prior years, but set to increase over

10% this year, driven by higher component costs.
Memory repricing: system DRAM goes from 7% of
datacenter capex in 2025 to 20% in 2026, and is
unlikely to come down materially.
CPUs are a smaller moving part: set to increase
from $64B this year to over $250B in 2030, driven
primarily by standalone deployments.
Total capex can exceed $4T in 2030:2.8x 2026
spending, with close to $1.3T of it on accelerators.
SEMICON TAIWAN 2026 | MARKET AND INDUSTRY TREND FORUM

電機機械  
上銀(2049.TT) 
AI 與半導體需求強勁，精密線性傳動元件交期持續拉長

AI 與半導體需求強勁，帶動工具機新一波的景氣循環，上銀生產
的精密線性傳動元件大量用於 AI 與半導體設備 ， 在半導體產業CapEx
確立的情況下，預期這波拉貨潮將延續至明年。在上一波 2021-22 年
的上升循環中，股價最高來到當年度 EPS 的 45 倍，考量到目前僅出
現第一波漲價，研究部以 2027 年 EPS 13.16 元給予 35x 本益比，目
標價為 460 元，給予買進的評等。

1. 半導體設備需求強勁，帶動工具機新一波的景氣循環 
上銀生產的線性滑軌與滾珠螺桿大量應用於 AI 與半導體設備當中，這
波景氣上升循環由從 4Q25 開始，拉貨動能逐季提升。從交期來看，線
性滑軌的交期從去年年底 3 個月，拉長到現在的 4~5 個月；滾珠螺桿
交期從去年年底 4 個月，拉長到現在 5~6 個月。從營收來看，扣除 2
月份有過年因素外 (去年過年在 1 月)，上銀的月營收 YoY 從 3 月的
17.4%一路提升到 8 月的 42.3%，此期間沒有縮小過，顯示營收不但在
成長，成長的動能還在加速。公司亦提到從 6 月開始 BB raio 大於 1，
需求非常強勁，已開始規劃明年過年期間要如何加班生產。研究部看好
這波由 AI 與半導體帶動的工具機需求延續至明年，預估上銀 2026/27
年營收為 300.1/378.2 億元，+23.7%/+26.0% YoY。

2. 市場供不應求，交期持續拉長看好後續漲價機會 
市場供不應求 ， 公司於今年3 月宣布調漲 7~15%， 已於6 月開始反應 。
2Q26 毛利率 34.2%，季增 2.2 個百分點，為稼動率與價格同步提升所
致，預估 3Q26 漲價效應完全反應後毛利率將進一步提升至 35.2%，研
究部預估上銀 2026/27 年的毛利率為 34.3%/35.6%， 分別年增5.5/1.3
個百分點，EPS 為 9.56/13.16 元，+121.7%/+37.6% YoY。 
這波由半導體產業 CapEx 所帶動的景氣上升，研究部看好在交期不斷拉長
的情況下，後續還有漲價、上修毛利率的機會。(上一波 2021-2022 年上升
循環中毛利率最高來到 37-38%)

一、 一、公司介紹 
   上銀科技成立於 1989 年 ， 專注於線性滑軌 、 滾珠螺桿與單軸機器人等精密線性
傳動元件的製造 ， 為該領域的全球領導廠商之一 。 除了持續研發線性傳動產品以外 ，
也自行開發出多種半導體自動化設備 ，產品廣泛應用於汽車 、 半導體 、 醫療 、 國防 、
航太及食品等產業。上銀憑藉自有品牌 「HIWIN」 的深厚研發實力與品質行銷全世
界，目前為全球第二大生產線性滑軌與滾珠螺桿的廠商，第一大為日商 THK 
(6481.T)。1H26 產品營收占比﹔線性滑軌 60%、 滾珠螺桿20%、 工業機器人12%、
其他 8%。2025 年地區營收占比﹔台灣 44%、中國 21%、德國 16%、美國 5%、
日本 4%、其他 10%。(地區營收 2025 年已是最新公布)

二、 產業分析 
上銀主要產品為線性滑軌與滾珠螺桿，產品功能如下﹔ 
1. 線性滑軌﹔一種線性傳動元件，由滑塊與滑軌組成，透過內部的滾珠或滾  
柱循環系統來降低摩擦，讓平台保持平行穩定的直線運動。  
2. 滾珠螺桿﹔一種線性傳動元件，由螺桿、螺帽與鋼珠組成，利用鋼珠在螺桿與
螺帽間循環滾動，將旋轉運動轉換為直線運動。

商業模式 
線性滑軌與滾珠螺桿為工具機零組件 ， 工具機產業商業模式分為以下三種 (以 AOI
檢測設備為例)﹔ 
1. 工具機零組件廠生產線性滑軌 ➨ 工具機 OEM 廠將各零組件組裝成五軸加工
機 ➨ 設備零組件廠用五軸加工機生產高精度定位平台  ➨ 設備商用高精度定位
平台與其他零組件組裝成 AOI 檢測設備。  
2. 工具機零組件廠生產線性滑軌 ➨ 工具機 OEM 廠將各零組件組裝成五軸加工
機 ➨ 設備商用五軸加工機自行生產所需的高精度定位平台或其他零組件。 
3. 工具機零組件廠生產的線性滑軌同時是工具機零組件與設備零組件  ➨ 設備
商直接購買線性滑軌來組裝。

### Page 5

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 5

產業趨勢 
JMTBA (日本工具機協會) 每月會將日本所有工具機 OEM 廠的訂單加總 ， 公布 「日
本工具機訂單金額」 ，其產品包含多軸加工機、CNC 車床、滾齒機……等等，由於
日本擁有全球規模前幾大的工具機 OEM 廠，如 DMG、Yamazaki、Okuma，且多
年來產品遍布各個產業所累積的經驗也使得這些公司不論在自研控制器的演算
法，還是機器長時間運轉下維持的精度與溫度都與其他國家有技術上的差距。  
因此，在各行各業景氣轉好，需要大量的工具機時，JMTBA 公布的訂單金額被視
為是工具機產業最重要的觀察指標，工具機 OEM 廠訂單增加 ➨ 增加向工具機
零組件廠的拉貨。 
觀察過去十年日本工具機訂單的走勢，可發現景氣大約呈現兩年上升、兩年下降
的循環。上一次的上升週期為 2021-22 年疫情加上塞港，全球各產業瘋狂拉貨，
使得工具機訂單 YoY 從 2020/11 的-6%，最高拉升到 2021/5 的 142%，一直到
2022/10 訂單 YoY 才轉負 。 塞港造成的恐慌式補庫存為工具機產業帶來20 年來不
曾有過的成長率 ， 也使得隨後各產業去化庫存時 ， 工具機這次的景氣休息得較久 ，
直到 2H25 才再次迎來上升週期。

圖六、日本工具機訂單及 YoY

資料來源：JMTBA

### Page 6

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 6

圖七、上銀過去十年股價走勢圖

資料來源：Wind 
（註：與日本工具機訂單 YoY 高度聯動）

而在此波的上升週期，生產線性滑軌與滾珠螺桿等工具機零組件全球市占前二的
廠商，THK、上銀一致提到這一次的需求來自 AI 與半導體，且需求非常強勁。 
細看 AI 與半導體產業，其中涵蓋了相當多工具機的蹤跡，舉凡伺服器散熱所需的
水冷板、快接頭及熱交換器，到伺服器機殼、Compute/GPU Tray 與各種結構件，
都需要以工具機進行切削或沖壓。半導體產業中，在製程升級與產能擴充的過程
需要大量的半導體設備 ，不論在前 、 中 、 後段的設備都須使用線性傳動元件 ， 如蝕
刻機、AOI 檢測設備、各類型 Bonder 等等。 
產品規格方面，AI 與半導體所需的工具機精度更高，使得 THK、上銀這種技術領
先的廠商進一步受惠 。 一般工業用工具機僅需使用H/N 級的線性滑軌與 C5/C7 級
的滾珠螺桿，生產水冷板與快接頭時需使用 SP/P 級的線性滑軌與 C3 級的滾珠螺
桿；在Die Bonder (晶粒鍵合機)與 Flip Chip Bonder (倒裝鍵合機)上則需使用 UP/SP
級的線性滑軌與 C0/C1 級的滾珠螺桿，生產高精度的線性滑軌與滾珠螺桿毛利率
顯著高於精度較低的。

SP ≈ 7 μm 
P ≈ 15 μm 
H ≈ 30 μm 
N ≈ 50 μm

在 AI 的快速發展下，半導體製程升級與產能擴張帶動半導體資本支出持續增加，

小結 
歷經 2022 年後的全球各產業庫存去化，工具機景氣委靡了接近三年的期間，如
今挾帶著 AI 伺服器與半導體設備需求強勁成長，再次迎來新一波景氣上升循環。
從水冷板、快接頭到各類半導體設備，皆帶動工具機需求快速升溫， 相關廠商也
同步指出需求強勁、訂單持續增加，交期明顯拉長。 
更重要的是，這波需求主要來自半導體設備等高精度應用領域，所需線性滑軌與
滾珠螺桿規格高於一般工業產品，上銀除受惠於工具機景氣重新向上外，亦可進
一步受惠於高規格精密線性傳動元件需求成長。

### Page 9

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 9

三、 三、營運狀況 
1. 1. 半導體設備需求強勁，帶動工具機新一波的景氣循環 
上銀生產的線性滑軌與滾珠螺桿大量應用於半導體設備當中，這波景氣上升循
環由從 4Q25 開始，拉貨動能逐季提升。從交期來看，線性滑軌的交期從去年
年底 3 個月 ，到五月法說會提到 3.5 個月 ， 再到八月份交期已經拉長到 5 個月 ；
滾珠螺桿交期從去年年底 4 個月，到五月法說會的 4.5 個月，再到現在的 6 個
月。從營收來看，扣除 2 月份有過年因素外 (去年過年在 1 月)，上銀的月營收
YoY 從 3 月的 17.4%一路提升到 8 月的 42.3%，此期間沒有縮小過，顯示營收
不但在成長，成長的動能還在加速。公司亦提到從6 月開始 BB raio 大於 1，需
求非常強勁，已開始規劃明 年過年期間要如何加班生產。研究部看好這波半導
體帶動的工具機需求延續至明年 ，預估上銀 2026/27 年營收為 300.1/378.2 億
元，+23.7%/+26.0% YoY。

圖十二、上銀各產品交期

資料來源：統一投顧整理

2. 2. 市場供不應求，交期持續拉長看好後續漲價機會 
市場供不應求，公司於今年 3 月宣布調漲 7~15%，已於 6 月開始反應。2Q26 毛
利率 34.2%，季增 2.2 個百分點，為稼動率與價格同步提升所致，預估 3Q26 漲價
效應完全反應後毛利率將進一步提升至 35.2%，研究部預估上銀 2026/27 年的毛
利率為 34.3%/35.6%，分別年增 5.5/1.3 個百分點 ，EPS 為 9.56/13.16 元，
+121.7%/+37.6% YoY。這波由半導體產業 CapEx 所帶動的景氣上升，研究部看
好在交期不斷拉長的情況下，後續還有漲價、上修毛利率的機會。 (上一波 2021-
2022 年上升循環中毛利率由 31%迅速拉升至 37-38%)

3. 預期景氣將延續至明年，目前評價偏低 
AI 與半導體需求強勁，帶動工具機新一波的景氣循環，上銀生產的精密線性傳動
元件大量用於 AI 與半導體設備，在半導體產業 CapEx 確立的情況下，預期這波
拉貨潮將延續至明年。在上一波 2021-22 年的上升循環中，股價最高來到當年度
EPS 的 45 倍，考量到目前僅出現第一波漲價，研究部以 2027 年 EPS 13.16 元給
予 35x 本益比，目標價為 460 元，給予買進的評等。

2382 廣達電腦(Quanta)

報 告 日 期 ：2026/09/08           研 究 員 ：曾 智 楷(Kevin  Tseng ) 
壹、投 資 建 議

一、 2Q26 營收 、獲利創高， EPS 7.43 元 
廣達 2Q26 營收 1.04 兆， QoQ +28.1% ，YoY +105.6% ，受 惠於
伺 服 器、 筆電 第二季 均 呈 現雙 位 數 的成長 。 產 品 組 合 部 分 ， 伺 服
器 佔 整 體 營 收 8 成 ， 其 中 ，AI 伺 服 器 對 整 體 伺 服 器 營 收 貢 獻約
75%~80% ，筆電 則佔 整體營收的 低雙位數百分比。 筆電部分，
2Q26 筆 電 出 貨 量1,150 萬 台 ，QoQ + 15% ，YoY  -5%。2Q26 筆
電 出 貨 優 於預 期 ， 主 要 由 於客 戶 新 機 種 的 拉 貨 動 能 延 續 至 第 二
季， 以及 各 品 牌 客 戶預期 記 憶 體 價 格 持 續 上 漲而 提 前 拉 貨。 而獲
利 方 面，2Q26 營 業 毛 利520 .8 億，QoQ +34.6 %。YoY + 46.6 %，
毛 利 率 5.0%，QoQ +0.2ppts，YoY -2.0ppts，營 業 利 益342 .4 億，
QoQ +48.7 %，YoY +67.8 %，營 利 率 3.3 %，QoQ +0.45 ppts，YoY 
-0.75 ppts 。公 司 表 示，高階 AI 機櫃對 公司 毛 利 的 稀 釋，隨著 產品
組合優化 影響 逐步 降 低 ， 以 及 2Q26 費 用 率 降 至 歷 史 新 低 的
1.72% ， 帶 動 營 利 率 獲 得 改 善 。2Q26 業 外 淨 利 23.2 億 ， 稅 後 淨
利 286.5 億， QoQ +35.2 %，YoY + 69.9 %， 單 季 稅 後EPS 7.43
元。

二、 3Q26  AI 伺服器動能延續，筆電面臨 逆風  
公司 公告 7 月 營 收 3399 億， MoM -6.3% ，YoY +120.7% ，今年
累 計 營 收2.21 兆， YoY +79.6% 。展望 3Q26 ， 公司 預期 AI 伺服
器 營 收 持 續 強 勁 增 長 ， 但 因 第 二 季 基 期 已 拉 高 ， 季 增 幅 度 會 低 於
第 二 季 。此 外 ， 公 司 表 示 ，第 三 季 起部 分 客 戶 專 案將由 Buy and Sell 
模 式 轉 為Consignment 模式，將 有 助 於 大 幅 減 輕 營 運 資 金 壓 力 並
舒 緩 毛 利 率 稀 釋影響 。 筆 電 部 分 ， 由 於 主 要客 戶 已 於 上 半 年 提 前
拉 貨，再加上 記 憶 體 與CPU 成 本 大 漲，壓 抑 終 端 採 購 意 願，預計
第 三 季 出 貨 量 將 季 減 逾20% 。 預估 3Q26 營收 1.05 兆 ，QoQ 
+1.5 %，YoY + 112.5 %， 毛 利 率 5.1%， 營 利 率 3.3%， 稅 後 淨 利
288 億 元 ， 單 季 稅 後EPS 7.46 元。

三、預估 2026/2027 年 EPS 27.50 /31.63 元 
展望 2026 年 ， 公 司 表 示 ，AI 伺 服 器 訂 單 能 見 度 已到 2028 年，
維持 今年 全年 AI 伺 服 器營 收 倍 增預期 不 變，而 通 用 伺 服 器亦 維持
全 年 營 收 雙 位 數 年 增 目 標 。筆 電 部 分 ，儘 管 上 半 年 連 續 兩 季 出 貨
表 現 優 於 預 期 ，但 由 於客 戶 提 前 備 貨且 缺 料 情 況在 下 半 年 未 見 明
顯改善 ，公司 預 估 下 半 年 出 貨 將 低 於 上 半 年 ，全 年 筆 電 出 貨 量 預

電 華 亞 廠，預 計 2027 年 第 四 季 完 成 交 割 點 交， 用 於2028 年台灣
AI 伺 服 器 產 能 倍 增 的 需 求 。預 估 公 司2026 全 年 營 收3.97 兆，YoY 
+87%，毛利率 5.0% ， 營 利 率 3.2%，稅後淨利 1062 億， YoY 
+41.7%，全 年 稅 後 EPS 2 7.50 元。預 估 公 司 2027 全 年 營 收5.04
兆，YoY + 27%，毛 利 率 4.8%，營 利 率 3.1%，稅 後 淨 利1221 億，
YoY + 15.0 %， 全 年 稅 後EPS 31.63 元。

四、投資建議為「買進」，目標價 440 元 
受惠 於 AI 伺 服 器 的 強 勁 需 求 ， 公 司 維 持今 年 AI 伺 服 器 營 收三位
數成長 展 望，且隨 美 系 四 大CSP 資 本 支 出 展 望 維 持 正 向，GPU 、
ASIC 伺 服 器專案 持續 放量 ，將 有 望 挹 注 公 司 營 運 持 續 增 長，對 公
司 長 期 展 望 維 持 正 向 。以公司 202 7 年 EPS 來看 ， 目 前 股 價 交 易
於 10~11 倍 本 益 比 ， 評 價 仍 具 上 修 空 間 ， 給 予 買 進 投 資 建 議 ， 目
標 價 為 440 元(14x 202 7 EPS) 。

貳、公 司 概 況

一. 公司簡介  
廣 達 電 腦(Quanta) 為 全 球 筆 記 型 電 腦 專 業 研 發 設 計 製 造 與 雲 端 運
算 解 決 方 案 領 導 供 應 商 ，為 全 球 前 五 大 筆 記 型 電 腦 代 工 廠 商 。 公
司 主 要 替 品 牌 業 者 從 事 代 工 服 務 ， 代 工 項 目 包 含 筆 記 型 電 腦 、 平
板 電 腦 、 智 慧 型 手 機 、 伺 服 器 、 商 用 及 家 用 智 慧 物 聯 網 裝 置 、 消
費 性 電 子 產 品 、 車 用 電 子 等 。 公 司 總 部 位 於 桃 園 龜 山 ， 生 產 基 地
包 含 桃 園 、 大 陸 上 海 、 重 慶 、 江 蘇 、 美 國 田 納 西 州 、 加 州 、 墨 西
哥 、 德 國 、 泰 國 等 。 公 司2025 年 伺 服 器 業 務(包含 AI 伺服器 )佔
整 體 營 收 比 重 近80% ， 筆 電(PC) 及 其 他 業 務 佔 約20% 。202 5 年
銷 售 地 區 比 重 ： 美 國73%、 大 陸 5%、 荷 蘭 2%、 日 本 3%、 其 他
17%。 筆 記 型 電 腦 主 要 客 戶 為Apple 、HP、Dell 、 華 碩 、 宏 碁 ，
伺 服 器 客 戶 包 含Microsoft 、Google 、Amazon 、Meta ， 而 車 用 客
戶為 Tesla 、GM 。 主 要 競 爭 對 手 為 鴻 海 、 緯 創 、 和 碩 、 仁 寶 電 腦
及 英 業 達 等 。

二. 營運概況及展望  
圖 一 、 廣 達 製 造 基 地 及 全 球 據 點

資 料 來 源 ：廣 達 、 宏 遠 投 顧 彙 整

1. 四大 CSP 上修 今年 資本支出達 7,450 億美元  
在 生 成 式AI 商 機 快 速 擴 張 帶 動 下，全 球 雲 端 基 礎 設 施 投 資 進 入 新
一 輪 高 峰 。 亞 馬 遜 、Alphabet 和 微 軟 認 為 其 在 先 前 的 鉅 額 投 資 已
帶 動 營 收 明 顯 成 長 ， 同 時 預 估 未 來AI 潛 在 商 機 仍 持 續 擴 大 。 其 中，
亞 馬 遜、Alphabet 和 Meta 已 陸 續 上 調2026 全 年 投 資 金 額，且 四
大雲端服務供應商 (CSP )總資本支出將上升至 7,450 億美元新高
規模 。 亞 馬 遜 、 微 軟 、Alphabet 與 Meta 2026 年第 1、2 季分別
為 1,306 億、1,701 億 美 元，同 時 亞 馬 遜、Alphabet 和 Meta 再上
修 2026 全 年 資 本 支 出 金 額，2026 年 資 本 支 出預 估 將達 7,450 億
美元 。而 根據 TrendForce 最 新 產 業 研 究，全 球 主 要 雲 端 服 務 供 應
商(CSP) 為 加 速 建 置AI 基 礎 設 施 ， 資 本 支 出 預 估 於2026 年 將 年
增 98%，2027 年 再 成 長50% 至 1.3 兆 美 元 左 右，年 增 率 收 斂的 主
要 原因， 除了 反 映 高 基 期 因 素之外 ， 部 分 可 歸 咎 為鉅 額 資 本 支 出
對 四 大 CSP 財 務 衝 擊 日 益 擴 大。然 而， 在 AI Inference 、Agentic 
AI 、 自 研 ASIC 和 新 一 代AI 模 型 持 續 推 升 算 力 需 求 的 情 況 下 ， 預
期 整 體 CSP 投 資 金 額 將 續 創 新 高 紀 錄 ， 產 業 成 長 有 望 從 原 先 以
GPU 擴 張 為 主，轉 為 包 含AI server 、液 冷 散 熱、先 進 封 裝、高 速
互 聯 、 電 源 和 記 憶 體 等 基 礎 設 施 全 面 升 級 的 態 勢 。

圖 二 、2024 -2027 年 全 球 九 大CSP 資 本 支 出 總 額 預 估(單位 :十 億 美 元)

資 料 來 源 ：TrendForce 、 宏 遠 投 顧 整 理

2. 2026 年全球 AI 伺服器出貨量預計年增 31%  
根據 TrendForce 最新 AI server 產 業 研 究 ， 近 期 超 大 型CSP 、
Tier -2 資 料 中 心 業 者 對NVIDIA GB/VR 等 整 櫃 式AI server 方 案 的
採 購 意 願 明 顯 提 高 ， 且Google 、AWS 新 一 代 ASIC 平 台 於 2026
下 半 年 陸 續 放 量，加 上 中 系 業 者 擴 大 採 用 本 地AI 方 案 以 滿 足 雲 端
大 型 語 言 模 型(LLM ) AI 需 求 ， 促 使TrendForce 上調 2026 年 AI 
server 出 貨 年 成 長 幅 度，從 原 先 的28% 更 新 為 近31% 。北 美 五 大
業者 2026 年 的 資 本 支 出 多 翻 倍 提 升 ， 反 映 出 大 型CSP 廠 持 續 擴
建 AI 資 料 中 心、GPU 叢 集、液 冷 散 熱 等 新 一 代 基 礎 設 施，以 應 對
生成式 AI 及 大 型 模 型 快 速 成 長 帶 來 的 算 力 需 求 。其中， Google 
2026 至 2027 年 都 將 側 重 自 家TPU 晶 片 發 展 ，2027 年 出 貨 量 有
望 繼 續 高 速 成 長 。AWS 2026 年以 NVIDIA GB300 作為 GPU AI 
server 主 力，自 研 ASIC 也 穩 定 出 貨，預 期 至2027 年 量 能 將 再 擴
大。 Meta 2026 年 主 要 採 用NVIDIA GB/VR 、AMD Helios 整櫃式
方 案，自 研 ASIC 布 局 預 計 至2027 年 將 明 顯 加 速，為 該 公 司 長 期
降低 AI 基 礎 設 施 成 本 、 提 升 推 論 效 率 的 重 要 策 略 。

3. 2Q26 PC 出貨量 年減 4.9% ，蘋果 市佔率逆勢擴張  
根據 國 際 數 據 資 訊(IDC )報 告 ，2026 年 第 二 季 全 球PC 出 貨 總 量 達
6,820 萬 台、年 減 4.9% ，主 要 由 於 記 憶 體 短 缺 持 續 推 升 零 組 件 成
本 ， 再 加 上 總 體 經 濟 環 境 不 佳 ， 導 致 終 端 需 求 與 換 機 意 願 大 幅 放
緩，整 體 拉 貨 動 能 無 力 延 續。其 中，Apple 2Q26 PC 出 貨 量 為670
萬 台 ，YoY +10.1% ， 主 要 受 惠 於MacBook Neo 帶 動 。 此 外 ， 面
對 零 組 件 短 缺 與 成 本 攀 升 逆 風 ， 蘋 果 憑 藉 龐 大 採 購 規 模 與 供 應 鏈
優 勢 穩 固 供 貨 ， 使 其 市 佔 率 逆 勢 擴 張 。

一、3Q26  AI 伺服器動能延續，筆電面臨 逆風  
公 司 公 告7 月 營 收 3399 億， MoM -6.3% ，YoY +120.7% ，今年
累 計 營 收2.21 兆， YoY +79.6% 。 展 望 3Q26 ， 公 司 預 期AI 伺服
器 營 收 持 續 強 勁 增 長 ， 但 因 第 二 季 基 期 已 拉 高 ， 季 增 幅 度 會 低 於
第 二 季 。此 外 ， 公 司 表 示 ，第 三 季 起 部 分 客 戶 專 案將由 Buy and Sell 
模 式 轉 為Consignment 模式，將 有 助 於 大 幅 減 輕 營 運 資 金 壓 力 並
舒 緩 毛 利 率 稀 釋影 響 。 筆 電 部 分 ， 由 於 主 要客 戶 已 於 上 半 年 提 前
拉 貨，再加上 記 憶 體 與CPU 成 本 大 漲，壓 抑 終 端 採 購 意 願，預 計
第 三 季 出 貨 量 將 季 減 逾20% 。 預估 3Q26 營收 1.05 兆， QoQ 
+1.5% ，YoY +112.5% ， 毛 利 率 5.1% ， 營 利 率 3.3% ， 稅 後 淨 利
288 億 元 ， 單 季 稅 後EPS 7.46 元。

表 現 優 於 預 期 ，但由 於 客 戶 提 前 備 貨且 缺 料 情 況在 下 半 年 未 見 明
顯改善 ，公司 預 估 下 半 年 出 貨 將 低 於 上 半 年 ， 全 年 筆 電 出 貨 量 預
估 將 較 去 年 呈 現 雙 位 數 下 滑 。而 營 利 率 部 分 ， 公 司 表 示 ， 由 於下

Airtac
Valuation
We derive our target price of NT$1,750 for Airtac by using a P/E methodology, as we believe the margin and earnings growth
outlooks will be the key drivers of the stock. We apply a P/E of 27.5x for 2H26-1H27E, which we derive from the mid range of
prior upcycle valuation 25-35x during 2021 in view of a strong upcycle with solid FA demand and ongoing share gains.
Risks
Key downside risks that could impede the Airtac stock from reaching our target price include: 1) a softer-than-expected China

macro economy, resulting in downside to our sales forecasts; 2) intensifying competition leading to gross margin pressures;
and 3) slower-than-expected progress of its linear guide business, causing low utilization of capacity.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

### Page 3

Hon Hai (2317.TW; Buy, on CL): Jul rev in line at +54% YoY; AI server and smartphones to support 3Q 
growth

Hon Hai’s Jul revenues were +54% YoY/ +15% MoM to NT$947m, or 2% higher than our 
estimates, overall in line. From a MoM perspective, Consumer Electronics & 
Components delivered significant growth MoM, benefitting from the pull-in 
momentum, followed by Cloud and Networking & Computing which achieved strong 
growth MoM. From a YoY perspective, all segments across Consumer Electronics, 
Components, Cloud and Networking & Computing delivered strong growth YoY, and 
management attributed this to the strong pull-in momentum and higher selling prices of 
Consumer Electronics and Computing products. We expect 3Q26E revenues to be +48% 
YoY / +22% QoQ to NT$3.1 trillion. June/ July delivered strong YoY (+52%/ 54% YoY), 
and we expect Aug revenues to remain strong at 73% YoY / 11% MoM to NT$1,051bn, 
supported by AI rack server ramp-up, and strong seasonality of the consumer 
electronics business. Maintain Buy (on CL).

Price Target Risks and Methodology – Hon Hai 
Valuation methodology: We are Buy rated on Hon Hai. Our 12-month target price of 
NT$400 is based on a 18.9x 2027E P/E multiple, which is set in line with peers’ PEG&M 
ratio (i.e., P/E vs. forward-year earnings growth and OPM). 
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected ramp-up of the AI server business; (2) 
weaker-than-expected EV total solution performance across EV assembly, design, 
software, and semis; (3) slower-than-expected ramp-up of capacity globally; and (4) 
fiercer-than-expected competition in the consumer electronics EMS business.

### Page 4

Quanta (2382.TW, Neutral): AI server in strong ramp up; PC to decline in 2H26 on high memory cost

Quanta’s Jul revenue was down 5% QoQ / up 131% YoY, or 21% higher than our 
estimate, which we attribute to the strong rack-level AI server shipments, along with the 
stronger than expected PC shipments at 4.5m units. Looking ahead, we expect 
sequential revenue decline in Aug-Sep, mainly on the memory price hike weighing on PC 
end demand. We model sequential revenue increases in Oct-Dec, supported by the next 
generation rack-level AI server shipments ramp up, which carries a higher ASP . Overall, 
we model Quanta’s 3Q / 4Q26E revenues to grow at 85% / 47% YoY, riding on the 
growing AI infrastructure trend. Maintain Neutral.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Quanta 
We are Neutral-rated on Quanta. Our 12-month target price of NT$299 is based on an 
11x 2027E P/E, which references PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E multiple 
correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s performance.  
Key upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market 
recovery; 2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for general servers.

### Page 6

AVC (3017.TW, Buy): Liquid cooling component ASP and GM upside on the rising complexity of server 
racks; July in line

AVC’s Jul revenue was up 57% YoY / 6% QoQ to NT$19bn, largely in line with our 
estimate. We attribute the strong YoY growth to the strong shipments of AI server liquid 
cooling components, riding on the increasing AI server end demand and a growing liquid 
cooling penetration rate. We model sequential revenue growth in Aug-Dec, considering 
(1) rising shipments of next-generation rack-level AI servers, (2) an increase in liquid 
cooling dollar content in next-generation AI server racks given their growing complexity, 
and (3) a rising liquid cooling penetration rate in both ASIC and GPU AI servers. Maintain 
Buy.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - AVC 
We are Buy-rated on AVC. Our 12-month target price for AVC is based on a near-term 
P/E, consistent with our Taiwan Technology coverage. We base our target price of 
NT$3,986 on a target P/E multiple of 28.2x applied to our forward-year EPS (2027E). 
Our target P/E is derived from the correlation between P/E and EPS growth of its peers 
in technology hardware.  
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected adoption of liquid cooling; (2) 
lower-than-expected demand for AI servers; (3) fiercer-than-expected competition; 
and (4) a slower-than-expected recovery in general servers.

### Page 7

Wiwynn (6669.TW, Buy): July revenues sequentially increase; Capex strong increase shows strong AI 
demand

Wiwynn July revenues were in line with our estimates at NT$118bn, driven by solid 
general servers growth and its major ASIC customers’ new generation of ASIC AI servers 
(air-cooling) entering mass production since June 2026. Looking ahead, we model 
sequential revenues decline in Aug and Sep, considering the high-base of general 
servers, while liquid-cooled ASIC AI servers of its major customer, and GPU AI server 
racks ramp up is in 4Q26E. Nevertheless, the 3Q26 revenues growth would be much 
stronger than 1Q26 (-5% QoQ), and 2Q26 (+1% QoQ), up to +22% QoQ, and we expect 
further acceleration to +32% QoQ in 4Q26, driven by AI servers new models ramp up, AI 
servers expansion from ASIC to GPU, potential ASIC AI servers SKU expansion, and less 
impact of business model transition from Buy & Sell to Consign for general servers. 
Maintain Buy.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Wiwynn 
We are Buy-rated on Wiwynn with a 12-month TP of NT$3,352.55. Our target price is 
based on 20x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E 
multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s performance. 
Key downside risks: 1) weaker-than-expected AI server demand growth; 2) 
slower-than-expected recovery in general server demand; and 3) more severe 
competition in the server ODM market.

### Page 8

Wistron (3231.TW, Buy): July and 2Q26 beat; AI server rack in a strong ramp-up with customer 
expansion and specification upgrades

Wistron’s July revenue was up 61% YoY to NT$308bn, or 30% higher than our estimate, 
reflecting strong AI server rack demand from neo-cloud and enterprises, offsetting the 
impact of the model transition. We expect sequential revenue growth ahead through 
Dec 2026, driven by new GPU AI server racks entering mass production in 4Q26, which 
will drive up the ASP , along with strong AI spending and the expansion of company’s 
customer base. The new GPU AI server racks have various racks across GPU, CPU, and 
LPU, supporting ODM growth in coming quarters. On PC, the company’s July shipments 
were at 1.6m units (-20% MoM / YoY), reflecting modest demand under high memory 
costs. Despite PC shipments likely being negative HoH considering the strong pull-in 
since late 2025, we expect the strong AI server rack ramp-up to offset the impact, 
driving 2H26 revenue up +28% HoH. Maintain Buy.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Wistron 
Our 12-month target price of NT$281 is based on a 13.1x 2027E P/E, which is derived 
from PC/server peer EPS growth vs. P/E multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a 
major factor for the stock’s share price performance.   
Key downside risks: 1) weaker-than-expected PC market recovery, 2) 
slower-than-expected AI server demand ramp-up, and 3) weaker-than-expected 
demand on general servers.

### Page 9

Gigabyte (2376.TW, Neutral): AI server in expansion; Strong capex commitment to capture the 
demand; July and 2Q26 beat

Gigabyte’s Jul revenue was up 60% YoY / 3% MoM, or 18% higher than our estimate, 
which we attribute to the strong shipments performance of both baseboard and 
rack-level AI servers. We model sequential revenue growth in Aug-Sep, supported by the 
rising AI server shipments, and we expect revenues to stay high in 3Q / 4Q26E post 
strong growth in 1H26, driving 2H26 revenues growth at +15% HoH. Management 
remains positive on 2H26 growth driven by strong AI demand, customers’ faster AI 
deployment, and Gigabyte’s expanding customer base and enhancing services. 
Management expects their customers’ infrastructure readiness and components 
procurement to continue to improve in 2H26, supporting the procurement on AI servers. 
Maintain Neutral.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Gigabyte 
We are Neutral rated on Gigabyte with a 12-month target price of NT$487. Our TP is 
based on a 14.7x 2027E P/E which is based on PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E 
multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s share 
performance. 
Key upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market 
recovery, 2) faster-/slower-than-expected gaming PC growth, and 3) 
faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up.

### Page 10

ASUS (2357.TW, Neutral): PC enjoying product mix upgrade; AI server client base expansion; July & 
2Q26 beat

ASUS’s Jul revenue was up 53% YoY / down 21% QoQ to NT$84bn, or 16% higher than 
our estimate, reflecting strong AI servers demand, along with PC product mix upgrade. 
Looking forward, we expect sequential revenue growth in Aug-Sep, considering the low 
base in Jul, followed by sequential revenue declines in Oct-Nov, as the rising memory 
cost would weigh PC end demand and AI servers model transition. Overall, our estimate 
shows double-digit YoY revenue growth for 3Q / 4Q26E, supported by PC product mix 
upgrade and rack-level AI server ramp up. Maintain Neutral.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Asus 
We are Neutral-rated on ASUS with a 12-month target price of NT$1,109. Our target 
price is based on 15.7x 2027E P/E, which is derived from brand makers’ average ratio of 
2027E P/E to 2028E NI YoY and OPM. We see EPS growth and OPM as major factors for 
the stock’s share performance.  
Key upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger / weaker-than-expected PC market 
growth; 2) faster / slower-than-expected AI and gaming PC growth; and 3) faster / 
slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up.

### Page 11

Compal (2324.TW, Neutral): AI server capacity in expansion; July better than expected

Compal’s Jul revenue was up 38% YoY / down 15% MoM to NT$81bn, or 10% higher 
than our estimate, reflecting AI servers shipment ramp up. Jul PC shipments was down 
22% YoY / 25% MoM to 1.8m units, reflecting the impact of 1H26 demand pull-in amid 
the rising memory cost. We model sequential revenue growth in Aug-Dec, considering 
the company’s rising AI server shipments, AI server capacity expansion, and PC product 
mix upgrade towards high end model (e.g. AI PC). Overall, we expect Compal’s 3Q / 
4Q26E revenues to grow at 6% / 10% QoQ, benefiting from the rising AI server end 
demand from Neocloud clients. We remain positive on Compal’s business diversification, 
supported by global capacity expansion in the US, Taiwan, and Vietnam, while consumer 
electronics still accounts for the majority of the company’s total revenue, which demand 
could be capped by rising raw material costs (e.g., memory, CPU). Maintain Neutral on 
fair valuation.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Compal 
We are Neutral-rated on Compal with a 12-month target price of NT$41.3. Our target 
price is based on 13.1x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth 
vs. P/E multiple correlation. We see EPS growth and market re-rating for ODM 
companies as major factors for the stock’s performance.  
Key upside/downside risks: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market recovery; 
2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger/weaker-than-expected demand for tablets.

### Page 13

Inventec (2356.TW, Neutral): AI server supporting growth ahead; Memory cost weighing on PC 
shipments; Jul Rev and 2Q26 beat

Inventec’s Jul revenue was up 66% YoY / down 12% MoM, or 32% higher than our 
estimate, which we attribute to the strong AI server end demand, while the MoM decline 
was mainly due to a decline in PC shipments, given the demand pull-in in 1H26. We 
expect sequential revenue decline in Aug-Sep, mainly on a decline in PC shipments from 
a consumption pull in amid rising memory costs. Meanwhile, growth momentum should 
recover in Oct-Dec, supported by AI server shipment ramp up. Overall, we expect the 
company to deliver double-digit YoY revenue growth in 3Q / 4Q26E, on rising AI server 
end demand and Inventec’s capacity expansion. We remain positive on Inventec’s AI 
server business expansion, while the rising memory cost would cap the PC shipments 
growth ahead. Maintain Neutral.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Inventec  
We are Neutral-rated on Inventec with a 12-month target price of NT$56.0. Our target 
price is based on 14.6x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth 
vs. P/E multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s 
performance. 
Key upside/downside risks: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market recovery; 
2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for general servers.

### Page 14

Pegatron (4938.TW, Sell): AI server driving 2H26 growth; PC growth capped by rising memory cost; Sell

Pegatron’s Jul revenue was +12% YoY / -6% MoM to NT$86bn, and we attribute the 
MoM decline to the sluggish PC end demand post the demand pull-in in 1H26, causing 
Pegatron’s PC shipments to decline to 0.40m units in Jul from 0.65m units in Jun. We 
model double digit MoM revenue growth in Aug-Sep, considering the low base in Jul, 
followed by modest sequential growth in Oct to Dec, mainly driven by the strong AI 
server shipments growth. Management remains positive on the momentum of AI server 
business in 2H26E, supported by the strong end demand from Neocloud and CSP clients. 
Overall, we expect Pegatron’s 3Q / 4Q26E revenues to grow at +20% / +27% YoY. We are 
positive on Pegatron’s AI server business expansion, while we expect the tight memory 
supply to cap the consumer electronics growth. Maintain Sell.

Earnings revision: Pegatron’s 2Q26 revenue, GM, and Opex ratio are largely in line with 
our estimate, while 2Q26 non-op income is higher than expected, leading 2Q26 net 
income 22% higher than our estimate. We factor in Pegatron’s Jul revenue and 2Q26 
result and raise our 2026-28E net incomes by 10% / 8% / 7%, mainly on higher revenues 
and lower opex ratios. We raise our 2026-28E revenues by 5% / 5% / 4%, mainly on 
higher contribution from AI server business. Our 2026-28E opex ratios are slightly 
revised up, mainly on higher R&D expense to support the AI server specification 
upgrades and PC product mix upgrade towards AI PC.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

### Page 3

Earnings revision: We factor in VPEC’s Jul-Aug revenues and raise our 2026-28E net 
incomes by 12% / 18% / 30%, mainly on higher revenues and GMs. 2026-28E revenues 
are revised up by 7% / 14% / 25%, mainly on higher contribution from optoelectronics 
business, driven by the strong end demand of AI data center optical networking, better 
InP substrate supply, and VPEC’s customers’ expansion and product expansion from CW 
lasers to EML, along with specification upgrade. Our 2026-28E GMs are revised up on 
rising contribution from optoelectronics business, which carries a higher margin than 
other product lines. We revise up 2027-28E opex ratios, mainly on higher R&D expense 
to collaborate with key customers on next generation optical networking products.

### Page 4

GS vs. Bloomberg consensus: Our 2026-27E revenues are higher than Bloomberg 
consensus, reflecting our positive view on VPEC’s optoelectronics business growth. Our 
2026-27E GMs are higher than Bloomberg consensus, mainly on product mix upgrade 
towards optoelectronics. Overall, our 2026-37E net incomes are 27% / 26% higher than 
Bloomberg consensus.

Valuation: We continue to derive target price from 2029E P/E discounted back to 2027E 
at 10.5% COE (no change), and derive target PE multiple from refreshed peers’ trading 
P/E vs. forward year fundamentals (NI YoY and OPM). Based on our updated 2029-30E 
NI YoY of 18% (vs. 20% previously) and 2029-30E OPM of 38% (vs. 37% previously), and

### Page 8

VPEC focuses on III-V compound semiconductor EPI wafer for microelectronics and 
optoelectronics devices based on InP and GaA substrate, and expands to EPI wafer for 
SiPh transceivers CW laser for data center applications. We expect strong momentum in 
VPEC growth driven by the SiPh ramp up in 2026E, microelectronics clients’ increasing 
demand, and its accumulated technology advantages on EPI wafer. We note that VPEC is 
currently trading lower than our TP implied 69x 2027E forward P/E, which is attractive, 
in our view.  
Price Target Risks and Methodology - VPEC 
Valuation: We adopt a discounted P/E method and apply a 22.2x target P/E on VPEC’s 
2029E EPS, discounting back to 2027E at a COE of 10.5% (beta 1.7x, risk free rate 1.6% 
and market risk premium at 5.1%), to derive our 12m TP of NT$793. Our target P/E is 
based on 0.4x PEG&M ratio derived from the average level of peers in the photonics 
supply chain. 
Key risk: Slower-than-expected SiPh business ramp up; weaker-than-expected 
microelectronics demand from smartphones clients; IDM offers EPI wafer in-house.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

policy constraints due to Goldman Sachs’ involvement in a transaction, and in certain other circumstances. Early-Stage Biotech (ES). An investment

rating and a target price are not assigned pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when this company has neither a drug, treatment or medical device that

3
Yageo
Valuation
We set our target price at NT$1,450, based on 35x average 2027E-28E earnings. We value Yageo based on PE as we believe the
company’s earnings performance and industry dynamics would be the key focuses of the market. Our target multiple of 35x is
at the higher end of the trading range since 2022. During this period the company has gone through the cycles, successfully
integrated the acquired business to enhance its product offerings, and started to deliver stable margins and earnings.

Risks
Key downside risks to our target price and rating include: 1) slower-than-expected demand recovery; 2) intensified peer and
pricing competition; 3) execution risks associated with M&A integration and synergies; and 4) FX and interest rate fluctuations.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

E Ink Holdings Inc.  | Asia Pacific
August  Sales +20% MoM/+3%
Y oY
In this report, we focus on E Ink's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price.
Details:
• August sales were NT$3,446mn (+20% MoM/+3% YoY).
• QTD sales reached 63% of our 3Q26 revenue estimate of NT$9,985mn (-2%
QoQ/-4% YoY) and 62% of consensus at NT$10,213mn (flat QoQ/-2% YoY).
Our view:
• The solid rebound in the Aug monthly sales should alleviate the potential

risk of missing full-year guidance again (now at 10-15% YoY for 2026), in our
view, as  3Q26 revenue is tracking in line with our estimate for  a modest
decline QoQ.
• While the worst seems to be behind us and valuation is at a trough level of
12x 2027 P/E, we see no strong catalysts in the near term, despite our
positive long-term view on  e-paper demand across various applications.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
E Ink Holdings Inc. (8069.TWO)
Base case, 12x 2027e P/E. We believe this valuation methodology best captures E Ink's earn-
ings growth momentum. We view our target multiple of 12x as fair, considering our estimated
17% earnings CAGR for 2025-28. The implied PEG of 0.7x is largely in line with its typical level
in 2022-25, when the stock was trading at an average P/E of 22x one-year forward consensus

EPS with a 37% earnings CAGR.
Risks to Upside
n Firmer-than-expected ESL orders
n Stronger demand for e-readers and e-notes
n Better macro conditions
n Milder competition
n Brisker inventory digestion
Risks to Downside
n Softer-than-expected ESL orders
n Slower demand for e-readers and e-notes
n Macro headwinds
n Intensified competition
n More protracted inventory digestion

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy ,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover . Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy , hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety , and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk -adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk -adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk -adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk -adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below .
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below .

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below .
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

MPI Corporation  | Asia Pacific
3Q Revenue Looks Better than
Feared; OW
MPI reported August sales of NT$2,048mn, +13% M/M and +70% Y/Y, above our
NT$1,876mn estimate –  mainly supported by price hikes, TPU, and other AI ASICs.
Assuming flat September revenue M/M, 3Q revenue would rise 13% Q/Q, above our
+7% estimate and the Street at +10% Q/Q.
Stay OW: As previously indicated, we expect 3Q revenue to exceed guidance given
CPC/VPC price hikes. With very strong micro bump demand and more migration to

2nm, we believe MPI needs to expand MEMS probe card capacity to at least 8–10mn
pins per month in 2027, positioning it to meet strong AI ASIC and GPU demand.
PCB material shortages remain a risk; however, MPI is diversifying suppliers and
seeking substitutes.
For CPO,  Insertion 2 qualification continues, with insertion 3 shipments on track to
start in 4Q26.
Investors were slightly frustrated by the conservative guidance, but still view MPI as
best positioned to benefit from increased chip probing demand over the longer term

Key Takeaways
The stock could come under pressure following management's conservative
guidance. However, we expect more updates to follow in coming quarters.
Strong micro bump demand and greater migration to 2nm lead the team to argue
that MEMS probe card capacity should expand to 8–10mn pins per month in
2027.
PCB material shortages remain an explicit risk. Mitigation centers on diversifying

suppliers/seeking substitutes to reduce reliance on constrained inputs.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
                        MPI Corporation (6223.TWO)                    
We derive our price target from a residual income model, as we believe this can best capture 
the long-term value. We assume a 9.78% cost of equity (risk-free rate 2.0% and risk premium 
6.0%), a payout ratio of 67%, a medium-term growth rate of 16.0%, and a terminal growth rate 
of 4.0%. Our price target implies 50x/25x 2027e/2028e P/E. We believe its valuation will con-
tinue to climb along with growing earnings.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

### Page 2

UMC (2303.TW)
11 September 2026 Citi Research
2
•2027 Pricing Looks More Favorable.  Management hopes to implement broader-based price increases across AI and
non-AI products to offset higher wafer, energy and other input costs and support new capacity investments. Pricing
should therefore turn more positive in 2027 if utilization remains high. Management noted that every 1% ASP
increase could improve gross margin by roughly 0.5–0.6ppt. However, margins are unlikely to revisit the >45%

COVID-era peak because depreciation is structurally higher following the current investment cycle.
•Intel 12nm cooperation: Capital-Light Path, contribution starting in late 2027 . The Intel collaboration is
strategically important because it gives UMC a capital-light path from its strong 22/28nm franchise into 12nm. Intel
provides existing Arizona manufacturing equipment and capacity, while UMC contributes technology conversion, RD
resources and its existing customer relationships. Initial customers are primarily Wi-Fi and communications-related,

with the first meaningful revenue expected around late 2027; broader contribution should build gradually thereafter
as customers validate the first wave. UMC expects to book the wafer revenue, with Intel manufacturing costs flowing
through UMC’s cost structure and economics broadly shared between the two parties. Expansion into smaller nodes
or advanced packaging with Intel remains possible, but management stressed that 12nm must first demonstrate

competitive yield, manufacturing performance and profitability.
•Silicon Photonics: Singapore Fab Becomes a Major AI Growth Platform.  The company is building dedicated 12-inch
capacity for silicon photonics in Singapore, with the new facility expected to be ready in roughly 14 months.  The
platform can encompass PICs and related components such as TIA, with management describing demand as
stronger than its available engineering resources.  UMC also has exposure to TFLN through an affiliate focused

initially on 6-inch technology, with potential migration to 8-inch manufacturing supported by UMC. Current
economics are attractive because photonics wafers can command multiples of conventional CMOS wafer pricing.
Longer term, UMC wants to combine silicon photonics, specialty materials and packaging capabilities into an
integrated platform.
•Advanced Packaging: Building an Open-Ecosystem Second Source. Advanced packaging represents UMC’s third
major structural growth driver. Rather than competing directly with TSMC’s fully integrated leading-edge ecosystem,

UMC aims to become a credible second-source, open-ecosystem provider. Its developing capabilities include
interposers, wafer-to-wafer and hybrid bonding, DTC, and related packaging processes, with silicon photonics
potentially integrated into the same broader solution. UMC is constructing two new advanced-packaging facilities in
Taiwan. Construction is expected to take more than two years, while existing facilities can provide limited capacity
during the interim.

•Specialty Strategy Provides Defense Against China Competition. UMC continues to differentiate itself through
specialty technologies rather than competing primarily on commodity mature-node capacity. Management
estimates 50–60%+ of revenue now comes from specialty processes, which provides meaningful insulation from
Chinese foundry pricing pressure. China’s semiconductor market is also becoming increasingly segmented into
“China-for-China” and “non-China-for-non-China” supply chains, limiting direct cross-border price competition.

UMC’s two China fabs remain highly utilized and represent roughly 10% of group capacity. Overall, UMC’s strategy is
shifting from being primarily a mature-node foundry toward a specialty and heterogeneous-integration platform
while improving utilization and pricing provide a stronger cyclical backdrop for 2027.

### Page 4

UMC (2303.TW)
11 September 2026 Citi Research
4
UMC
Valuation
Our target price for UMC of NT$125 is based on 15x 2027E EPS, which is higher than the midpoint of its 3-year forward PE. We
believe our target multiple is justified by our belief that UMC should benefit from the robust demand in 22/28nm, TSMC's
increasing order outflow opportunity, long-term opportunity in advanced packaging and photonics and that its niche
technology offerings should help it outperform its trailing-edge foundry peers. Our target price equates to 2026E/27E PB of

3.5x/3.2x.
Risks
Key downside risks to our target price include: 1) a longer-than-expected demand recovery; 2) more competition from peers in
28nm; 3) worse-than-expected developments from the company's China investment; 4) a significant decline in smartphone
end-demand; 5) poor contribution from Novatek's iPhone OLED business; and 6) fiercer ASP competition from Chinese peers.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

iPhone 18 Pro and Pro Max: (1) Cooling: the new iPhone is embedded with a more 
powerful SoC (A20 Pro) and Apple Intelligence, making thermal management essential 
to the device. The next generation of Vapor Chamber is used in the new iPhones, which 
we see as positive for the cooling suppliers. Read more: AVC, AAC; (2) Camera: variable 
aperture is adopted in rear cameras, in which lens aperture changes based on the focal 
length, allowing easier user control of DoF (depth of field), taking pictures in low lighting, 
or having a more natural broken gradient background when in portrait mode. Read 
more: Largan. Selling price is higher by US$100 (or high single-digit %) compared to 
iPhone 17 Pro series, with the same delivery time.  
Apple Watch and Airpods: Airpods 5 feature active noise cancellation, and are 
designed to allow more natural interaction (e.g. transparency mode, better conversation 
awareness). The wireless charging case models are US$30 cheaper compared to Airpods 
4. Apple Watch 12 and Ultra 4 are priced the same as the previous generation with 
better specifications: health sensing system with the most accurate heart rate sensing in 
a wearable, Siri recap, audio intelligence, etc.

## Document 113: 260910_gs_TSMC

Source PDF: all report pdf/260910_gs_TSMC.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-10
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Presenters: Wendell Huang, SVP & CFO  
Bottom Line: We have three key takeaways: (1) TSMC continues to view AI as a 
multi-year megatrend that remains at a very early stage, with lower compute costs 
expected to accelerate AI adoption and ultimately drive greater demand for 
advanced silicon; (2) execution in Arizona continues to progress well, with Phase 1 
achieving yields comparable to Taiwan and already generating profits, while TSMC 
expects approximately 30% of its N2-and-beyond capacity to be located outside 
Taiwan in five years, primarily in Arizona; and (3) despite dilution from the N2 ramp 
and overseas expansion, management remains comfortable achieving its long-term 
gross margin target of 56% and above, supported by disciplined capacity planning, 
high utilization, productivity improvements, cost reductions, and pricing that reflects 
TSMC’s value. 
Key Takeaways

AI remains in the early stages of a multi-year megatrend: TSMC continues to n
see increasing adoption of AI. Management also highlighted the shift from 
generative AI toward agentic AI, which should broaden compute demand 
beyond accelerators to CPUs. TSMC remains confident in the durability of the AI 
cycle based on discussions not only with its direct customers but also with its 
customers’ customers. Recent visits of US CSPs further reinforced management’s 
confidence, as cloud service providers appeared well prepared in areas including 
power and broader infrastructure deployment. 
Lower compute costs should drive greater consumption and silicon demand: n
Management believes open-source models and continued improvements in 
computing efficiency will reduce token costs, leading to greater AI 
consumption and broader adoption, supporting incremental demand for 
advanced silicon. TSMC has already incorporated this dynamic into its capacity 
planning and continues to expand aggressively to support what it views as

### Page 2

structural, multi-year demand growth.
Disciplined demand validation remains critical amid aggressive expansion: n
TSMC uses a combination of top-down and bottom-up analysis to validate demand.
Its top-down assessment considers macroeconomic conditions, semiconductor
demand, fabless/system companies, the foundry industry, etc, while its bottom-up
approach evaluates demand across different segments and incorporates feedback
from customers and customers’ customers. Management acknowledged that

semiconductors will remain cyclical, but believes correctly identifying the underlying
megatrend is more important than attempting to forecast every short-term
fluctuation.
Arizona execution is demonstrating that TSMC’s manufacturing model can n
travel: TSMC reiterated that overseas expansion is based on customer demand for
geographic flexibility and an appropriate level of government support. Arizona
Phase 1, using N4 technology, is in operation, with yields comparable to Taiwan,

satisfactory quality and reliability, and positive profitability. Management views
this as an important milestone because it demonstrates internally and to customers
that TSMC can produce wafers in the US with the same quality as in Taiwan.
Equipment move-in for Phase 2, which will use N3 technology, is expected to begin
soon; construction of Phase 3 has started; and preparation work for Phase 4 and the
first US advanced-packaging facility is underway.

Arizona has substantial room for further expansion: In addition to its original n
Arizona development, TSMC has acquired another parcel of land capable of
accommodating an additional five to six fabs, supporting its recently
announced incremental US$100bn investment. Based on its current plan, TSMC
expects approximately 30% of its N2-and-beyond capacity to be located outside
Taiwan in five years, primarily in Arizona. However, the most advanced technologies

will continue to ramp first in Taiwan because the early-stage technology transfer
requires close collaboration and frequent movement of engineers among R&D and
manufacturing sites. Overseas deployment will generally follow after the technology
has stabilized in Taiwan.
Customers increasingly value and are willing to pay for geographic flexibility: n
Management noted that customers initially requested only a modest amount of
geographically diversified supply, but their requirements have increased as TSMC has

demonstrated successful execution in Arizona. TSMC believes this geographic
flexibility represents incremental value and intends to reflect that value in its pricing,
with customers increasingly willing to recognize the associated premium.
Technology, manufacturing excellence, capacity, and trust underpin TSMC’s n
competitive moat: Management believes customers select a foundry partner based
on access to the best technology, the ability to ramp rapidly into high-volume

manufacturing with attractive economics, sufficient capacity, and confidence that
the foundry will not compete with its customers. TSMC is confident in maintaining
this lead by continuing to advance its roadmap. Management also highlighted its
record of rapidly ramping new technologies, the scale of its wafer and
advanced-packaging investments, and its consistent delivery against customer
10 September 2026   2
Goldman Sachs TSMC (2330.TW)For the exclusive use of KEVINLU@LENOVO.COM

e92c7a75ab8b4efbba794e6b187208c8

### Page 3

commitments as key elements of customer trust that cannot be replicated through
CapEx or engineering recruitment alone.
Pricing remains strategic rather than opportunistic: TSMC does not intend to raise n
prices simply because near-term utilization is high, nor reduce prices simply because
utilization temporarily weakens. Instead, the company’s approach is to price
sustainably and consistently based on the value it provides, while ensuring that
customers can remain successful over the long term. Management noted that

TSMC’s structural profitability has continued to improve, with its long-term gross
margin target increasing from 50% five years ago to 53% and now 56% and above.
Leading-edge wafer and advanced-packaging capacity remain tight: n
Management continues to build capacity wherever possible to meet customer
demand. N3 demand remains particularly strong: unlike prior technology cycles,
when demand for a node typically began declining two to three years after its

introduction, TSMC is adding further N3 capacity and still does not expect supply
to be sufficient. The company is also expanding advanced-packaging capacity to
address strong accelerator demand. Outside TSMC, management identified power
and memory as potential bottlenecks cited by other parts of the AI supply chain,
while emphasizing that foundry capacity itself remains difficult to expand because
fabs require two to three years to build and another one to two years to ramp.

TSMC’s disclosed AI revenue understates its broader exposure: Management n
reiterated that its disclosed AI revenue definition is relatively narrow and primarily
captures GPUs, custom AI accelerators and memory base die. It excludes CPUs and
networking products because TSMC cannot reliably determine whether each chip is
ultimately deployed in an AI system. As agentic AI drives incremental demand across
x86, Arm, and RISC-V CPUs, as well as networking and other supporting silicon,

TSMC’s total AI-related exposure is therefore broader than its reported
AI-accelerator revenue. The company intends to retain its existing definition until it
can more accurately distinguish the end use of these products.
No visible barrier to continued transistor scaling: TSMC is currently ramping N2, n
with A14 expected approximately two years later, while development of the
subsequent node is already underway. Management does not currently see a

technical issue preventing delivery of the next node or continued advancement
beyond the existing roadmap. TSMC views close collaboration with customers as a
key foundry advantage, allowing the company to develop each technology against
specific customer performance, power, density, and economic requirements.
Cross-node capacity optimization is an underappreciated advantage: TSMC n
highlighted its ability to generate additional output through productivity

improvements and to convert tools and capacity across technology nodes. For
example, the company can use portions of its N5 capacity to support N3 demand
and can also optimize capacity across less-adjacent nodes where technically
feasible. Management views this cross-node flexibility as a distinctive element of
TSMC’s manufacturing excellence, allowing the company to respond more
quickly to demand changes while reducing the risk of capacity obsolescence.

Advanced packaging is strategic primarily because it enables wafer growth: n
10 September 2026   3
Goldman Sachs TSMC (2330.TW)For the exclusive use of KEVINLU@LENOVO.COM
e92c7a75ab8b4efbba794e6b187208c8

### Page 4

CoWoS capacity remains tight, and TSMC already started outsourcing selected 
packaging processes to OSAT partners to support additional growth. Management 
emphasized that advanced packaging is intended primarily to ensure and enable 
wafer demand rather than serve as a standalone profit pool. TSMC is therefore 
developing multiple packaging architectures, including panel-level packaging, etc, 
while remaining open to customers using alternative packaging providers, as either 
approach ultimately supports demand for TSMC wafers. 
Advanced-packaging execution remains a key differentiator: TSMC noted that its n
current advanced-packaging capability supports approximately 5.5x reticle size, 
with a roadmap to reach approximately 14x reticle sizes within the next several 
years. While competing roadmaps may also appear attractive, management believes 
execution and the ability to deliver in high-volume manufacturing will ultimately 
determine competitive outcomes. TSMC does not believe competitors can use 
advanced-packaging wins to meaningfully displace its leading-edge wafer 
business, as foundry and packaging remain fundamentally different businesses. 
A unified TSMC culture supports overseas manufacturing execution: TSMC’s n
objective is not to create separate Taiwan and US cultures, but to establish a 
consistent TSMC culture centered on integrity, commitment, innovation, and 
customer trust (ICIC). Locally hired Arizona employees spend six to eighteen months 
training and working in Taiwan, while experienced Taiwan-based employees are 
assigned to Arizona to work alongside local teams. Over time, the number of Taiwan 
assignees should decline as locally trained employees assume greater responsibility. 
TSMC is applying this approach across its overseas manufacturing sites. 
Management remains comfortable with its 56%-and-above long-term gross n
margin target: TSMC reiterated its 6 major drivers of profitability: new-technology 
ramp, pricing, cost, product mix, utilization, and FX. The N2 ramp is expected to 
dilute 2026 GM by approximately 2-3ppts, but its profitability should improve over 
time toward the corporate average; management also noted that N2’s structural 
profitability is better than N3 at a comparable stage. Overseas expansion is 
expected to dilute GM by 2-3ppts during the early portion of the current five-year 
period, then widening to 3-ppts later as more overseas fabs begin production. Each 
new fab will initially create dilution before improving along its learning curve. Taking 
these puts and takes together, management remains very comfortable that 
disciplined capacity planning, healthy utilization, cost reduction, productivity 
gains, and pricing that reflects TSMC’s value will enable the company to deliver 
for shareholders and achieve its long-term gross margin target ‘of 56% and 
higher’ with focus on the ‘higher’ side. 
Valuation/Risks

TSMC (Buy): Our 12-month price target is NT$3,100, which is derived by applying a 
target P/E multiple of 22x to our 2027E EPS. Our 22x target P/E is benchmarked against 
1.0stdv above its 5-year trading average. For the ADR (TSM), we have a 12-month price 
target of US$620, based on a USD/TWD rate of 30.0 and an ADR premium of 20%. Key

downside risks to our views: (1) further deterioration in end-demand recovery

yields/execution resulting in worse-than-expected profitability; (5) stronger 
competition resulting in ASP/profitability erosion; and (6) unfavorable FX trend or 
higher-than-expected cost increase weighing on the margin outlook.

季獲利走勢與年成長率

研究員/陳晏平 TINA.YP.CHEN@uni-psg.com

1H26 稅後淨利為 438 億元，成長 328%YOY，EPS 為 1.69 元。獲利
成長主要因:(1)台新銀核心收益皆逾兩成的成長 ， 該子公司獲利成
長 26%YOY 至 124 億元；(2)2025/7/24 完成合併新光金，新光銀、
元富證 1H26 合計額外貢獻 67 億元獲利，加上 2026 年接軌 IFRS 
17，壽險開始有穩定利差益，1H26 合併後壽險子公司的獲利為 225
億元。展望 2026~2027 年，由於:(1) 台新銀維持放款將高個位數
成長，並上修淨利差擴大幅度，上修 2026 年淨利息收益至成長
19%YOY，並預期 2027 年將成長 5%YOY；(2) 台新銀財管商品銷售
動能強勁，信用卡業務持續成長，預期 2026 年整體淨手續費收益
將雙位數成長 ，2027 年個位數增加 ；(3) 台新銀資產品質穩定 ， 但
為新增放款 1%/1.5%法定提存，預期2026~2027 年淨提存將增加六
成至 30 億元 ；(4) 新壽接軌 IFRS 17 後擁穩定利差益 ， 另有80~100
億元的收購價格分攤數額外挹注，推估 2026 年將貢獻獲利逾 400
億元 ，2027 年亦將持續穩增；(5) 台股價量齊揚 ， 帶動證券子公司
獲利明顯增長，但同時也墊高基期。因此，在全年的合併效果下，
預估台新新光金 2026~2027 年稅後淨利為 805 億元、798 億元，成
長 116%、 衰退1%YOY， 扣除特別股股利25 億元後 ，EPS 為 3.11 元、
3.08 元 。 投資建議方面 ， 雖2026 年獲利明顯跳增 ， 且新壽1H26 已
累積 512 億元 OCI 資本利得亦可為配息來源，預期 2027 年現金股
利將優於 2026 年的 1 元水準 ， 不過2026 年獲利提升有部份是來自
無實際現金流入的 PPA，加上人壽能否上繳股利至金控尚不明確，
因此，在銀行、證券子公司較能上繳股利情況下，現金股利提升幅
度恐將受限。另以2027 年每股淨值 30 元、ROE 10~11%來看，目前
股價已逾 1.3xPBR，評價相對合理，故對台新新光金持「中立」投
資建議。

重點摘要： 
1. 台新銀放款將高個位數成 長，並上修淨利差擴大幅度，上修
2026年淨利息收益至成長19%YOY， 並預期2027年將成長5%YOY 
1H26底台新銀放款餘額為2.027兆元，成長11%YTD、12%YOY，
主要成長動能來自法人金融 、 加值型房屋貸款 、 個人無擔信用
貸款，分別增加15.5%、10%、15%YOY，一般房屋貸款則 小增
2%YOY。利差方面，雖高利差外幣放款成長動能強勁，1H26外
幣放款成長33%YOY，有提高資金運用效率 ，但受到台灣市場資
金緊俏影響，導致資金成本上揚，因此，2Q26存放利差為

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

1.53%，縮小6bpsQOQ、擴大14bpsYOY，淨利差亦同步 縮小
6bpsQOQ、擴大13bpsYOY至1.42%。不過因放款YOY持續成長、
淨利差YOY亦呈現擴大趨勢，1H26台新銀淨利息收益為200億
元，成長27%YOY。

圖一、台新銀利差趨勢

資料來源： 公司、統一投顧彙整

展望2026~2027年，由於出口動能維持強勁，景氣持續成長，
台新銀維持原預期—法金放款雙位數增長 、個金放款穩健高
個位數成長 ， 預期台新銀2026年整體放款將高個位數成長 。但
因2027年即將與新光銀合併 ， 預期將有部分法金客戶重疊 ， 須
重新評估放款額度 ， 預期2027年放款成長性將會不如2026年。
利差方面 ， 雖近期台灣資金緊俏影響資金成本 ， 但因先前美國
降息刺激高利差外幣放款需求，在 1H26存放款結構調整優於
預期下，公司上修2026年淨利差擴大幅度至5~8bpsYOY(原預
期將擴大4~5bpsYOY)。 因此 ， 研究部上修台新銀2026年淨利息
收益2%至404億元，成長19%YOY，並預期2027年將續增5%YOY。

2. 台新銀財管商品銷售動能強勁，信用卡業務持續成長，預期
2026年整體淨手續費收益將雙位數成長，2027年個位數增加 
1H26台新銀整體淨手續費收益為110億元，成長32%YOY，主要
成長動能來自:(1)投資市場火熱刺激財富管理商品熱銷，整
體財富管理業務手續費收益成長32%YOY；(2)透過簽帳金額結
構改變(減少低/零手收)、控制權益支出，信用卡業務手續費
收入增加28%YOY；(3)隨企金放款增長，企金相關手續費增加
56%YOY。

展望2026~2027年，因投資市場持續位在高檔，仍有利財富管
理商品銷售 ， 因此 ， 公司維持2026年台新銀整體淨手續費收益

3. 台新銀資產品質穩定，但為新增放款1%/1.5%法定提存，預期
2026~2027年淨提存將增加六成至30億元 
2026年以來無大額不良壞帳產生 ， 台新銀1H26底逾放比 、 呆帳
覆蓋率分別為0.13%、923%，資產品質不錯，些微優於產業平
均逾放比0.14%、呆帳覆蓋率940%。提存方面，雖針對新增逾
放的額外提存不多 ， 但因放款成長動能不弱 ， 而有新增放款的
1%或1.5%正常提存需求 ， 台新銀1H26淨呆帳提存為25億元 ，增
加112%YOY。

圖二、台新銀資產品質趨勢與同業平均水準比較

資料來源： 公司、統一投顧彙整

展望2026~2027年，目前資產品質穩定，暫無潛在大型不良放
款產生 ， 但因仍持續有新增放款的1%或1.5%正常提存， 公司預
期台新銀2026年信用成本將在10~15bps區間(維持不變)，較
2025年的10bps略增。研究部按公司預期的區間中上緣進行推
估，估計台新銀2026~2027年淨呆帳提存為30億元，較2025年
增加近六成。
